## Baseline

Baseline coverage recorded over the full `tests/` tree with `python3 tools/replay_coverage_diff.py baseline --out .aw/state/trimprobe-g00ljy/base -- tests/`:

```
Baseline recorded to .aw/state/trimprobe-g00ljy/base/baseline.json
head: f3afece925f521dd5a6e930e02b584a2c08cf950
xdist: True
functions: 4583
subprocess_only: 100
wall_s: 1199.10
```

- Baseline `head` equals measured HEAD `f3afece925f521dd5a6e930e02b584a2c08cf950`.
- Pytest collection summary from `python3 -m pytest -o addopts= -q --collect-only -p no:randomly -m "not slow and not livecorpus" tests/ > .aw/state/trimprobe-g00ljy/collect.txt`:
  ```
  8261/8666 tests collected (405 deselected) in 18.79s
  ```

## Method

The classification script `.aw/state/trimprobe-g00ljy/classify.py` parses each candidate key in `keys.tsv` and classifies them in strict order:
1. `key-gone` if the module file is absent or `tools.replay_coverage_diff.extract_functions` finds no span for the qualname in the HEAD module.
2. Operator check: if `tools.mutation_probe.apply_operator` returns `None`, replace with the first accepted entry of `tools.mutation_probe.OPERATORS`; if none accepted, `unprovable` with reason `no applicable operator`.
3. `unguarded-unreached` if absent from `baseline.json` (Reason: "unreached by the non-slow selection at f3afece925f521dd5a6e930e02b584a2c08cf950").
4. `subprocess-only` (partition `P4`) if `subprocess_only` is true.
5. `reached` if test contexts exist. Each context is mapped to a selector using the longest-existing-file-prefix rule of `tools.mutation_probe._node_id_to_selector`, and matched against `collect.txt` to compute `N` and `est_s = 10 + 0.48 * N`. Reached keys are sorted by `(N, key)` ascending and assigned to `P2` while cumulative `est_s <= 7200s`, with the remainder assigned to `P3`.
6. For every key, its positive control file is derived from `hpjj44` `## Derived properties` `Reaching Test Files` (highest exclusive lines, ties broken by path ascending, verified with `git show 19313eed^:<path>`).

Script source:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Classify the 319 budget-exhausted keys against baseline.json and collect.txt at HEAD."""

import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

repo_root = Path.cwd().resolve()
sys.path.insert(0, str(repo_root))

from tools.replay_coverage_diff import extract_functions
from tools.mutation_probe import apply_operator, OPERATORS, _node_id_to_selector

scratch_dir = repo_root / ".aw/state/trimprobe-g00ljy"
keys_path = scratch_dir / "keys.tsv"
baseline_path = scratch_dir / "base/baseline.json"
collect_path = scratch_dir / "collect.txt"
hpjj44_path = repo_root / ".aw/records/research/20261013-uncitedaudit-01-hpjj44-uncited-trim-audit.findings.md"

def load_hpjj44():
    with open(hpjj44_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        lines = f.readlines()
    in_table = False
    hpjj44_map = {}
    for line in lines:
        if '## Derived properties' in line:
            in_table = True
            continue
        if in_table:
            if line.startswith('## '):
                break
            if line.startswith('| `agent_workflows/'):
                parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split('|')[1:-1]]
                key = parts[0].strip('` ')
                files_cell = parts[1]
                matches = re.findall(r'`([^`]+)`\s*\((\d+)\s+exclusive lines', files_cell)
                candidates = []
                for path, excl in matches:
                    candidates.append((int(excl), path))
                candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (-x[0], x[1]))
                hpjj44_map[key] = candidates
    return hpjj44_map

def get_control_file(key, hpjj44_map):
    if key not in hpjj44_map or not hpjj44_map[key]:
        return "none"
    for excl, path in hpjj44_map[key]:
        res = subprocess.run(["git", "cat-file", "-e", f"19313eed^:{path}"], capture_output=True)
        if res.returncode == 0:
            return path
    return "none"

def context_to_selector(ctx: str) -> str:
    if "." not in ctx:
        return ctx
    module_part, test = ctx.rsplit(".", 1)
    node_id = f"{module_part}::{test}"
    return _node_id_to_selector(node_id, str(repo_root))

def run_classification():
    if not baseline_path.is_file():
        print(f"Error: {baseline_path} not found")
        sys.exit(1)
    if not collect_path.is_file():
        print(f"Error: {collect_path} not found")
        sys.exit(1)

    with open(keys_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        keys_data = [line.strip().split("\t") for line in f if line.strip()]
    assert len(keys_data) == 319, f"Expected 319 keys, got {len(keys_data)}"

    hpjj44_map = load_hpjj44()

    with open(baseline_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        baseline = json.load(f)
    baseline_funcs = baseline.get("functions", {})
    head_sha = baseline.get("head", "")

    # Load collect.txt lines
    with open(collect_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        raw_collect_lines = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]
    # Filter only test node ids (lines containing '::' and ending with test name / parameter)
    collect_lines = [l for l in raw_collect_lines if "::" in l and not l.startswith("NOTE:") and not l.startswith("=")]

    # Map collect lines for efficient lookup
    # A selector matches a line if line == selector or line.startswith(f"{selector}[")
    # To make this fast, build an index or prefix match
    collect_set = set(collect_lines)

    def count_matches_for_selector(sel: str) -> int:
        if sel in collect_set:
            exact = 1
        else:
            exact = 0
        # Check parametrized variants
        prefix = f"{sel}["
        param_count = sum(1 for l in collect_lines if l.startswith(prefix))
        return exact + param_count

    module_source_cache = {}
    module_funcs_cache = {}

    classified_records = []
    unmapped_contexts = []
    operator_replacements = []

    for key, op, old_rc in keys_data:
        mod_path, qualname = key.split("::", 1)
        control_file = get_control_file(key, hpjj44_map)
        file_obj = repo_root / mod_path

        # 1. key-gone check
        if not file_obj.is_file():
            classified_records.append({
                "key": key,
                "operator": op,
                "operator_note": "original",
                "reach_class": "key-gone",
                "N": "n/a",
                "est_s": "n/a",
                "surviving_files": [],
                "control_file": control_file,
                "partition_or_verdict": "key-gone",
            })
            continue

        if mod_path not in module_source_cache:
            try:
                src = file_obj.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                module_source_cache[mod_path] = src
                spans = extract_functions(src, mod_path)
                module_funcs_cache[mod_path] = {s.qualname for s in spans}
            except Exception:
                module_source_cache[mod_path] = ""
                module_funcs_cache[mod_path] = set()

        if qualname not in module_funcs_cache[mod_path]:
            classified_records.append({
                "key": key,
                "operator": op,
                "operator_note": "original",
                "reach_class": "key-gone",
                "N": "n/a",
                "est_s": "n/a",
                "surviving_files": [],
                "control_file": control_file,
                "partition_or_verdict": "key-gone",
            })
            continue

        src = module_source_cache[mod_path]

        # 2. Operator check
        chosen_op = op
        op_note = "original"
        if apply_operator(src, qualname, op) is None:
            accepted_op = None
            for candidate in OPERATORS:
                if apply_operator(src, qualname, candidate) is not None:
                    accepted_op = candidate
                    break
            if accepted_op is None:
                classified_records.append({
                    "key": key,
                    "operator": op,
                    "operator_note": "no applicable operator",
                    "reach_class": "unprovable",
                    "N": "n/a",
                    "est_s": "n/a",
                    "surviving_files": [],
                    "control_file": control_file,
                    "partition_or_verdict": "unprovable",
                })
                continue
            else:
                operator_replacements.append((key, op, accepted_op))
                chosen_op = accepted_op
                op_note = f"replaced from {op} to {accepted_op}"

        # 3. unguarded-unreached check
        if key not in baseline_funcs:
            classified_records.append({
                "key": key,
                "operator": chosen_op,
                "operator_note": op_note,
                "reach_class": "unreached",
                "N": "n/a",
                "est_s": "n/a",
                "surviving_files": [],
                "control_file": control_file,
                "partition_or_verdict": "unguarded-unreached",
            })
            continue

        # 4. subprocess-only check
        b_info = baseline_funcs[key]
        if b_info.get("subprocess_only"):
            classified_records.append({
                "key": key,
                "operator": chosen_op,
                "operator_note": op_note,
                "reach_class": "subprocess-only",
                "N": "n/a",
                "est_s": "n/a",
                "surviving_files": [],
                "control_file": control_file,
                "partition_or_verdict": "P4",
            })
            continue

        # 5. reached
        ctxs = b_info.get("contexts", [])
        selectors = []
        sel_files = set()
        total_N = 0

        for ctx in ctxs:
            sel = context_to_selector(ctx)
            selectors.append(sel)
            if "::" in sel:
                fpart = sel.split("::", 1)[0]
                sel_files.add(fpart)
            cnt = count_matches_for_selector(sel)
            if cnt == 0:
                unmapped_contexts.append((key, ctx, sel))
            total_N += cnt

        est_s = round(10.0 + 0.48 * total_N, 2)
        surviving_files = sorted(sel_files)

        classified_records.append({
            "key": key,
            "operator": chosen_op,
            "operator_note": op_note,
            "reach_class": "reached",
            "N": total_N,
            "est_s": est_s,
            "surviving_files": surviving_files,
            "control_file": control_file,
            "partition_or_verdict": None, # assigned below
        })

    # Partition reached keys
    reached_records = [r for r in classified_records if r["reach_class"] == "reached"]
    reached_records.sort(key=lambda r: (r["N"], r["key"]))

    cum_est_s = 0.0
    p2_records = []
    p3_records = []

    for r in reached_records:
        if cum_est_s + r["est_s"] <= 7200.0:
            cum_est_s += r["est_s"]
            r["partition_or_verdict"] = "P2"
            p2_records.append(r)
        else:
            r["partition_or_verdict"] = "P3"
            p3_records.append(r)

    p2_est_s_sum = sum(r["est_s"] for r in p2_records)
    p3_est_s_sum = sum(r["est_s"] for r in p3_records)

    # Check capacity warning for P3
    p3_overflow_keys = []
    p3_cum = 0.0
    for r in p3_records:
        p3_cum += r["est_s"]
        if p3_cum > 7200.0:
            p3_overflow_keys.append((r["key"], r["est_s"], p3_cum))

    p4_records = [r for r in classified_records if r["partition_or_verdict"] == "P4"]
    p4_records.sort(key=lambda r: r["key"])

    final_verdict_records = [r for r in classified_records if r["partition_or_verdict"] not in ("P2", "P3", "P4")]
    final_verdict_records.sort(key=lambda r: r["key"])

    queue_ordered = p2_records + p3_records + p4_records + final_verdict_records
    assert len(queue_ordered) == 319, f"Total records {len(queue_ordered)} != 319"

    # Save classified data as json
    with open(scratch_dir / "classified_summary.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({
            "p2_count": len(p2_records),
            "p2_est_s": round(p2_est_s_sum, 2),
            "p3_count": len(p3_records),
            "p3_est_s": round(p3_est_s_sum, 2),
            "p4_count": len(p4_records),
            "final_verdicts": Counter(r["partition_or_verdict"] for r in final_verdict_records),
            "unmapped_contexts": unmapped_contexts,
            "operator_replacements": operator_replacements,
            "p3_overflow_keys": p3_overflow_keys,
            "records": queue_ordered,
        }, f, indent=2)

    print("=== CLASSIFICATION SUMMARY ===")
    print(f"Total keys: {len(queue_ordered)}")
    print(f"P2 count: {len(p2_records)}, sum est_s: {p2_est_s_sum:.2f}s")
    print(f"P3 count: {len(p3_records)}, sum est_s: {p3_est_s_sum:.2f}s")
    print(f"P4 count: {len(p4_records)}")
    counts = Counter(r["partition_or_verdict"] for r in queue_ordered)
    for p, c in sorted(counts.items()):
        print(f"  {p}: {c}")
    print(f"Sum: {sum(counts.values())}")
    print(f"Operator replacements: {len(operator_replacements)}")
    print(f"Unmapped contexts count: {len(unmapped_contexts)}")
    if p3_est_s_sum > 7200.0:
        print(f"P3 CAPACITY WARNING: {p3_est_s_sum:.2f}s > 7200.0s ({len(p3_overflow_keys)} overflow keys)")
    else:
        print(f"P3 within 7200s headroom ({p3_est_s_sum:.2f}s)")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_classification()
```
